ASEAN経済は国ごとにばらつき。ベトナムは輸出や
鉱工業生産の好調を背景に高成⾧持続。フィリピン
では食料品やエネルギー価格高騰が消費の重石に。
タイでも原油高を背景にインフレ圧力が強まる
APAC
賃金改善が続く一方、個人消費の回復に弱さ。実
質消費支出は前年比減少が継続。消費者マインド

も小幅悪化し、物価高への警戒が根強い。景気動
向指数は低下したものの、基調判断は改善を維持
日
本
先週（10/5～10/12）発表の主な経済指標
米国では金融政策運営の変化と財政懸念、欧州では財政不
安が金利上昇圧力に。欧州では⾧期金利上昇による景気下
押し圧力が、ECBの追加利上げの必要性を低下させる可能性
金利・コスト上昇などの逆風下でも、設備投資意欲は旺盛。家
計の節約志向や企業間の格差は残るものの、価格転嫁・賃上
げも継続しており、日銀の想定するシナリオに沿った動き

米国：中間選挙を目前に住宅市場は軟調、アフォーダビリティへの不満が共和党への逆風に
 住宅ローン金利は足元で再び上昇
― 借入コストの負担増により、足元の住宅ローン申請件数は減少
 物価上昇が生活コストを押し上げるなか、金利上昇による住宅取得環境の悪化も家計の不満を助長する要因に
― トランプ大統領の生活費への政策対応は約7割が「期待より悪い」と評価しており、支持率は低下基調
―11月に控える中間選挙では、アフォーダビリティへの対応が引き続き主要な論点

米国：金融政策・財政の両面から米長期金利に上昇圧力
 住宅ローン金利の基準となる米国10年債利回りは9月以降水準を切り上げ、足元（10/6時点）では5.3％近傍に
― 構成要素をみると、追加利上げ観測の高まりを受け、実質予想短期金利が上昇。ウォーシュFRB議長の就任以降、フォ
ワードガイダンスの後退を背景に、経済指標への金利の感応度が高まっている可能性
― 加えて、足元ではタームプレミアムも拡大。中間選挙を控えた家計支援策への思惑などから、財政懸念が意識されてい

る模様。トランプ大統領は10/2、Medicare加入者2,000万人超への90ドルの一時金支給を発表

欧州：長期金利上昇を受け、一部ECB高官は追加利上げの必要性低下を指摘
6
 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と
目されるルペン氏（国民連合）が財政赤字を縮小する意欲を示し、拡大はひとまず一服
―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す
 長期金利の上昇は需要を抑制。ECB高官からは追加利上げの必要性が低下するとの見方も
― エネルギー高によるインフレ圧力と金利上昇による景気下押し圧力が併存。ECBの政策判断は一段と難しくなる可能性
仏長期金利上昇の経済影響（左）と直近のECB高官発言（右）
（出所）LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 8

7
日本：金利・コスト上昇下でも企業活動は底堅く、設備投資意欲の強さを再確認
 10月のさくらレポートではAI関連需要の拡大とその裾野の広がりを背景に、企業活動の底堅さを確認
― 同一の企業コメントに登場するテーマの組み合わせを集計した共起分析では、「金利×設備投資」の組み合わせが増加。
金利上昇への意識が強まる一方、成長・省人化投資の旺盛なニーズは継続
 賃上げや資産効果が消費を下支えする一方、物価高を背景とした消費の二極化が継続
― 企業は価格転嫁を積極化するも、消費者の節約志向が根強く、商品競争力や顧客層によって転嫁余力に格差
⇒景気・物価は強弱入り混じるも、日銀の想定するシナリオに概ね沿った展開。12月利上げ見通しは不変
企業コメントの共起関係の変化
（注） 同一の箇条書きコメントに、双方のテーマのキーワードがある場合を1件として計上。
なお、テーマ間の因果関係を意味するものではない
（出所） 日本銀行「地域経済報告」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
企業コメント概要
0 10 20 30

金利 × 設備投資
価格転嫁×企業収益
AI関連 × 設備投資
中東情勢 × 価格転嫁
節約志向 × 消費・販売
7月
10月
（件）
設備投資における金利負
担、企業収益における価
格転嫁の重要性を示唆
 AI関連需要は想定を上回るペースで拡大。半導
体製造装置に加え、工作機械や光ファイバー関連
製品など幅広い分野に波及
 金利・建設コストの上昇下でも、競争力維持に必
要な投資を継続。一方、採算悪化や調達コスト増
から投資を縮小・延期する動きも
設備投資・生産
 原材料・人件費の上昇を受け、企業の価格改定は
機動化。値上げ頻度を引き上げる企業がみられる
一方、消費者の価格感応度も高まる
 価格転嫁が進み賃上げ原資を確保できる企業が
ある一方、転嫁不足から利益を削って賃上げを続
ける企業も
価格転嫁・賃金
 株高による資産効果や賃上げを背景に、高額品・
高機能家電・旅行などの需要は堅調
 一方、物価高の長期化を受け、高年層を中心に生
活防衛意識が強まる。低価格品へのシフトや値上

世界経済：エネルギー・食料の供給ショックによる負担は、所得階層や国によって格差
 生活費高騰に伴うインフレの影響は所得階層により格差
― 食料・エネルギー支出の割合が高い低所得層は負担が大。IMFは低所得層に絞った所得移転が望ましい対応と指摘
 エネルギー・食料の供給ショックによる影響は国家間でも格差
― 先進国では、エネルギー供給ショックによる消費の下押し効果は低所得層で特に大きい
― 食料品への支出割合が高い新興国・低所得途上国では、食料供給ショックによる消費の下押し効果が相対的に大きい

 タイでは過去にも大規模洪水が発生。2011年には、日系自動車企業の部材調達が滞り、日本国内生産に影響した経験も
―2011年の洪水発生時には、工場の被災や物流停滞、人員不足が発生。タイの製造業稼働率は一時30％近く低下
― 当時浸水した工業団地では日系企業約450社が被災。タイ製部品の調達難により、日本でも完成車が一時減産に

―10月頭時点では各社、大規模な設備被害や減産は見込まず、日本の部材調達への影響も限定的となる見込み
― ただし、10月中旬にかけ更なる大雨が予想されており、操業停止の長期化や被害地域の拡大には依然警戒が必要

日本：個人消費は耐久財特需が剥落。今後は良好な所得環境を背景に緩やかに拡大
12
 8月の消費活動指数は、耐久財特需の剥落などにより、前年比＋0.9％と伸びが鈍化
― 耐久財消費は春先の自動車特需の反動から減少。食品などの非耐久財も、根強い節約志向を背景に弱含みが継続
― 底堅く推移してきたサービス消費も、台風・大雨や猛暑による外出控えの影響などから拡大ペースが鈍化
 今後は良好な所得環境が消費を下支え。個人消費は緩慢なペースながら、底堅く推移する見通し
― 実質所定内給与はプラス圏の伸びを維持しており、基調的な所得環境は良好。増加基調の購買力が消費を下支え
― ただし、インフレ率の再上昇で消費の回復ペースは緩慢。マインドの改善が一服したことも回復ペースを抑える要因に

よる海外消費（アウトバウンド消費）を加えるための調整要因。アウトバウンド消費は
SNAの家計最終消費支出に含まれるが、GDPの計算上は輸入として控除される

日本：景気ウォッチャーは消費の緩やかな持ち直しを示唆も、家計・企業とも先行きには慎重
13
 家計動向（現状判断DI）は改善。9月の消費回復を示唆するも、回復ペースは緩慢
― 特にサービス関連の上昇が顕著。8月に増勢が一服したサービス消費は、大型連休や秋の行楽需要を受け持ち直し
― ただし、先行き判断DIは低下しており、個人消費全体でみれば緩やかな回復であることを示唆
 一方、企業動向は、現状・先行きとも慎重。景気回復の勢いは強くない
― 企業動向（現状判断DI）は製造業・非製造業ともに低下。AI関連など需要は堅調も、資材高などの供給制約が重石に

― 先行き判断についても、原材料高や金利上昇、中東情勢の不透明感などが重石となっている模様

本稿では下記3つの疑問を解説。
Q1. 足元までの金利上昇は「良い金利上昇」か「悪い金利
上昇」か？

か？

「悪い金利上昇」のどちらに向か
うかで、成⾧戦略、技術革新、
財政規律、市場環境が論点と
なる。

市場焦點
美國
• 10月S&P Global綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖較前月回落，但仍處高檔區間；服務業動
能略為放緩，製造業PMI則升至56.0，創近4年半新高。新訂單增速加快 ，顯示企業需求、投
資及庫存回補動能仍具韌性 ，然而企業售價與投入成本同步上升 ，反映需求改善 、供應鏈延
遲及成本轉嫁效應持續存在 ，通膨降溫進程仍具不確定性 。整體而言 ，市場關注焦點轉向通
膨與利率走勢，高利率環境恐維持較長時間，進而推高股債資產評價波動。
中國
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季 GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期 ，在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，
短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建議聚焦在
半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費 、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，

仍需觀察後續刺激政策與消費信心回升情況。
法國
政局動盪
• 法國金融市場因各地抗議活動持續擴大 ，面臨股債雙跌的局面。市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支
出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮 。法國10年期公債殖利率一度突破 5%，法德利差亦擴
大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標仍處於歷史相對低檔 ，遠低於歐
元區主權債務危機期間，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務危機的風險仍相對有限。

美國PMI維持強勁擴張，經濟成長續航但通膨壓力再升溫
• 企業需求強勁支撐美國景氣維持高檔擴張 ：美國10月綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖略低於前月，但仍處於近五年來
的高位區間，顯示企業活動持續高速擴張；其中服務業 PMI為57.9、製造業PMI升至56.0並創53個月新高，新訂單增速
更創逾4年半最佳表現，反映內需穩健、出口需求改善與企業補庫存效應同步發酵，美國第四季經濟動能仍具韌性。
• 成本上升與供應瓶頸推升通膨風險： 企業售價漲幅創2022年8月以來最高，能源、運輸、金屬原料及薪資成本同步上揚 ，
加上供應鏈交期延長與訂單積壓擴大，使企業得以持續轉嫁成本，暗示未來數月消費者通膨可能再度偏離聯準會目標區
間，市場對利率維持較高水準的預期恐因此升溫。
• 後市觀察：景氣韌性有利獲利成長，但通膨可能推升殖利率、壓抑高評價資產。投資配置建議優先布局美國大型科技股 、

美國金融股及工業資本支出受惠族群，可透過NASDAQ 100 ETF、S&P 500 ETF及美國銀行股ETF參與企業獲利成長；
固定收益則以短天期投資級債為主，以降低利率波動風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.24

海外資金流入美國市場，支撐美國資產吸引力
• 海外資金持續流入美國：美國財政部8月TIC資料顯示，
整體資金淨流入 1,998億美元，其中民間部門淨流入
1,785億美元，為主要資金來源；外資淨買進美國長
期證券3,007億美元，遠高於美國投資人加碼海外資
產216億美元，反映全球資金在經濟成長與流動性考
量下，對美國金融市場仍維持高度的配置需求。
• 全球資金偏好美國資產： 外資 8月淨買入美國股票
1,319億美元，高於美國公債與公司債 ，顯示海外投
資人仍看好美國企業獲利與經濟基本面 ，雖然短期國
庫券持有量下降，部分跨境美元資金亦出現波動，但
以10月近況來看長天期公債標售仍吸引境外央行 、
保險資金及大型資產管理機構參與，反映高殖利率水
準對長期資金仍具吸引力。在經濟成長維持韌性與企

業獲利支撐下，美國資產仍為全球資金配置的重要核
心市場。
• 市場看法：海外資金流入有助反映美國資產吸引力，
惟後續仍須觀察通膨 、Fed政策及企業獲利表現 。投
資布局建議可以美國投資等級債、美國中短天期公債
及大型AI基礎建設受惠族群為核心配置 ，兼顧收益、
防禦及長期成長潛力。

歷史數據顯示全球股市突破歷史新高後，後續上漲機率高
• 很多人直覺以為見高就是頂，但過去20年的統計資料顯示，全球股市在創下新高後，多數時間選擇繼續向上走。
• 勝率顯著偏高： 全球股市 (MSCI全球指數 )創新高後持有一年 ，獲得正報酬的機率高達 62%，三個月後的正報酬機率更達
71%。美股與台股表現最亮眼，標普500指數創高後一年上漲機率為76%，那斯達克為79%；而台灣股市表現更為驚人，創
高後三個月內上漲的機率高達82%。
• 下檔拉回風險有限：在創高後短期(一個月內)修正幅度其實相當溫和 ，全球多數主要市場的回徹幅度中位數僅 1%～2%左右。
• 突破前高優於連續創新高： 若市場在歷經至少一個月以上的整理後突破前高 ，其未來一年的平均報酬率高達 8.4%，遠高於

連續每天創新高的3.9%。這代表市場經適度整理後的突破，具備更健康的上漲動能。
創新高後的平均遠期報酬率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.22

顧
市場寬度收窄，由少數巨頭獨撐
• 當前的市場特徵由少數巨頭帶頭衝： 科技股權重較高的指數(如那斯達克、台股、韓股)，長期以來都有少數權重股拉動指數
的現象。自2024年以來，全球13個主要市場中有10個市場在創新高時，參與上漲的股票比例明顯下降 。已開發市場創新高
時高於200日均線的股票比例從過去 75.5%降至65.1%，新興市場更降至 61.3%。這意味著指數看似風光 ，但多數個股並未
同步創高，當漲勢過度集中於少數大型科技巨頭時，整體市場的抗風險能力會相對減弱。
• 小結與投資建議：歷史經驗顯示，創新高本身是強勢市場的特徵，後續繼續走揚的機率遠大於立刻崩盤。然而，鑑於目前市
場寬度收窄、權重高度集中於科技股的現狀 ，盲目追高個股的風險已顯著增加 。投資建議可以利用ETF掌握大盤趨勢，由於

指數上漲主要由龍頭股驅動 ，透過市值型ETF(如追蹤標普500ETF)能直接享受龍頭巨頭帶來的指數創高紅利 ，避免選錯個股
而賺了指數、賠了價差。此外，提防短期股市回檔，可運用定期定額分批布局或等待大盤適度拉回整理再行加碼，降低追高
風險。
創新高時不同水準的市場寬度佔比

法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓
• 法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓：法國各地抗議活動持續擴大，最初由學生發起，隨後因生活成本高漲而引發其
他群體響應。在財政空間有限、俄烏戰爭與美伊衝突推升能源價格的背景下，政府難以有效緩解民生成本壓力，法國金融市
場因此面臨股債雙跌的局面。
• 公債殖利率攀升，但系統性風險仍相對可控：市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮。法國10
年期公債殖利率一度突破5%，法德利差亦擴大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標（CISS）仍處於
歷史相對低檔，遠低於歐元區主權債務危機期間，顯示金融市場尚未出現廣泛的系統性壓力，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務
危機的風險仍相對有限。

日本央行本週料維持利率不變
日本央行利率走勢預估
資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.15 資料來源： tradingeconomics，2026.10.21
日本貿易收支
• 日本貿易逆差擴大，進口增幅高於出口：日本2026年9月貿易逆差擴大至5,366億日圓，較去年同期的2,777億日圓增加93%，
高於市場預期的1,810億日圓，連續第五個月呈現貿易赤字。出口年增16.7%至10.97兆日圓，受惠於半導體相關電子零件、
製造設備及汽車出口成長；惟進口年增18.9%至11.51兆日圓，增幅高於出口，主要受到原油、半導體電子零件及液化天然
氣進口增加帶動。其中，原油進口年增51.1%，部分反映日本在荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張下，積極分散能源採購來源。
• 日本央行本週料維持利率不變：日本央行9月將政策利率調升25個基點至1.25%，創31年來新高。考量前幾次升息對經濟的

傳導效果仍待觀察，加上貿易逆差擴大反映進口成本壓力，多數分析師預期日本央行本週將暫停升息，以評估貨幣緊縮對經
濟與通膨的影響。

顧
中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期：顯示中國經濟在第二季落底後呈現溫和回升 ，從季度表現來看，
一、二、三季增速分別為5.0%、4.3%及4.5%，反映政策支持與外需復甦開始發揮效果 ，全年達成官方4.5%成長目標的機率
明顯提升，第三季經濟改善的主要動能來自出口 ，受惠於全球企業持續擴建 AI基礎設施，及科技產品需求強勁 ，中國高科技
產品出口表現亮眼 ，尤其是積體電路 、伺服器、電腦零組件，及儲存設備等產品增幅顯著 ，高技術製造業維持雙位數成長 。
同時，中國對美國及歐盟出口維持強勁 ，推升貿易順差 走升，為支撐經濟的重要力量 。然而，內需疲弱仍是當前經濟最大隱
憂，房地產調整尚未結束，居民消費與企業投資意願保守，使經濟復甦結構呈現「外熱內冷」的特徵。

• 展望後市：在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建
議聚焦在半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，仍需觀察後續刺激政
策與消費信心回升情況。
資料來源：開源證券，2026.10.22

彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高
• 彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高：彭博產業研究認為，中國AI產業未來將由大型科技平台主導 ，其中騰
訊、字節跳動與阿里巴巴憑藉龐大用戶基礎 、數據資源、資金實力及完整生態系統 ，在AI競賽中具備明顯優勢 ，這些企業可將
AI廣泛應用於電商、社群、遊戲、金融與雲端業務，提升營運效率並強化競爭門檻，例如：阿里巴巴利用 AI 驅動電商平台上的
個人化產品推薦，而騰訊則運用 AI 優化短影音廣告並強化微信功能。騰訊自 2026 年 7 月起開始將混元 AI 模型整合至其電子
遊戲中，使遊戲角色及助理能更準確地回應玩家 ，然而，AI模型訓練與基礎建設投入龐大 ，獲利能力短期仍受壓抑 ，且須面對
監管、資料隱私及晶片供應等挑戰 。此外，智譜、月之暗面、MiniMax及商湯等AI新創雖具技術創新能力，但缺乏規模效益、

穩定現金流及長期資本支持，未來獲利挑戰較大。
• 投資建議：看好中國AI長期發展者，可優先關注具資金 、算力與應用場景優勢的大型平台企業 ，受惠程度可望高於多數 AI新創
公司。

台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高，台股第四季有望先蹲後跳
• 台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高： 9月外銷訂單1,142.1億美元，連兩個月破千億美元 ，再創單月新高；年增率高達 62.4%，連續
20個月呈雙位數正成長 。經濟部表示，AI強勁需求超乎預期 ，預估今年第四季接單還會優於第三季 。展望10月表現，外銷
訂單將持續亮麗，估落在1,125億至1,159億美元，有望連三個月破千億美元。
• 台股第四季有望先蹲後跳： 10月初以來，台股在盤中觸及歷史高點 49,968點後面臨一波修正 ，主要反映在新一輪的財報公
布前，市場觀望AI投資是否存在泡沫化風險 。短期來看，台股在季線附近出現支撐 ，不過在11月初的美國期中選舉落幕前 ，
台股維持區間震盪的可能性較高；中期來看，美國期中選後往往因政治不確定性下降，歷史統計股市上漲機率高，加上台灣

年底集團作帳及選舉行情，正向看待年底台股表現。

美國財政壓力仍高，房市降溫與通膨回升增添經濟不確定性 
經濟 
⚫ 8 月美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1,668 億美元，年減 51.6%，主因收入增
加、支出下降，但部分支出提前至7 月支付，影響單月表現。累計前11
個月赤字仍近 2 兆美元，關稅收入轉正，惟利息支出持續攀升，財政壓
力仍高。 
⚫ 8 月美國成屋銷售年率降至 398 萬戶，月減 2.0%、年減 1.2%，為 2025
年 6 月以來首度跌破 400 萬戶，顯示高房貸利率持續抑制房市需求。房
價中位數為 42.91 萬美元，年增 1.6%，連續 38 個月上漲；待售成屋庫
存增至 162 萬戶，相當於 4.9 個月供給量，創逾 10 年高點。整體而言，
成屋市場呈現銷售放緩、房價續升與庫存增加態勢，供給改善有助市場
逐步趨於平衡。 
⚫ 8 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%，高於上月 0.1%，年增率維持
3.4%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，年增率則降至 2.4%。能源價格由跌轉升，
其中汽油上漲 3.9%， 住房月增0.3%， 為推升通膨主因 ； 食品月增0.1%。
整體而言，短期物價壓力升溫，但核心通膨仍持續降溫。 
⚫ 8 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)月增 0.4%、年增 5.4%，高於上月的 0.1%
及 4.8%，主要反映能源價格回升影響，其中柴油價格大漲24.1%；服務
價格則受運輸及倉儲成本增加推升而續呈上揚。核心 PPI 月增 0.3%，
低於前月的 0.4%，顯示生產端通膨壓力仍具韌性，惟基礎通膨動能尚
未明顯升溫。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期美加貿易衝突持續升溫 ， 加拿大自9 月 8 日起對美國商品加徵 15%
至 50%報復性關稅 ；美國則擴大限制部分加拿大乳製品 、酒類及機車產
品輸美，並限制加拿大企業參與部分政府採購，雙方爭端已由關稅戰延
伸至市場准入領域，增添北美供應鏈及貿易前景不確定性。 
⚫ 美國國會推進對俄羅斯新制裁法案 ， 擬授權總統對俄羅斯能源主要購買
國商品課徵最高 100%關稅，以壓縮俄方能源收入。法案雖獲參議院高
票支持，但眾議院前景仍不明，且可能波及中國、印度及部分盟友，引
發貿易與外交疑慮。

一、 美國—財政壓力仍高，房市降溫與通膨回升增添經濟不確定性 
2026 年 8 月(2026 財年第 11 個月)美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1,668.0 億美元，
較去年同期 3,447.9 億美元縮減 51.6%；當月政府收入約 3,600.3 億美元，年增
4.6%，政府支出約 5,268.3 億美元，年減23.5%，惟部分原定 8 月初支付的軍人薪
資與退休金、退伍軍人福利、補充保障收入及部分醫療保險給付款項因 8 月 1 日
適逢非工作日而提前至 7 月支付，使單月支出受到付款時點影響。 收入方面，8
月關稅淨收入約 128.4 億美元，扭轉 7 月關稅淨收入為負 85.5 億美元的情況；累
計前 11 個月關稅淨收入約 1,673.1 億美元，較去年同期增加 1.3%。 支出方面，
8 月淨利息支出約 856.1 億美元，累計前 11 個月達約 1 兆 169.7 億美元，年增
8.9%，顯示利息負擔持續增加。 累計 2026 財年前 11 個月，美國聯邦政府財政
赤字約 1 兆 9,655.9 億美元，較去年同期小幅縮減 0.4%，其中收入年增 3.3%至約
4 兆 8,454.5 億美元，支出年增 2.2%至約 6 兆 8,110.4 億美元。整體而言，8 月單
月赤字雖明顯縮減 ， 但部分受到付款時點移轉影響 ， 且累計赤字仍接近2 兆美元 ，
加上利息支出持續攀升，美國聯邦財政壓力仍維持高檔。 
2026 年 8 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率降至 398 萬戶 ， 較上月減少2.0%，
較去年同期下降 1.2%，並為 2025 年 6 月以來首次跌破 400 萬戶，顯示高房貸利
率持續抑制房市交易動能。區域表現方面，西部銷售持平，東北部、中西部及南
部分別月減 4.0%、3.1%及 1.6%；房價方面，成屋售價中位數為42.91 萬美元，年
增 1.6%，連續 38 個月上漲。供給端則持續改善，待售成屋庫存增至 162 萬戶，
月增 3.2%、年增 5.9%，相當於 4.9 個月供給量，為近 10 多年來最高水準，住房
可負擔指數亦由去年同期的 101.2 升至 104.7。整體而言，8 月美國成屋市場呈現
銷售放緩、房價續漲及庫存增加的格局，高利率仍抑制成交動能，惟供給增加及
可負擔性改善，有助提升購屋者的選擇與議價空間，並促進市場逐步趨於平衡。 
2026 年 8 月美國消費者物價指數(CPI)經季節調整後月增 0.4%，高於上月的
0.1%，年增率維持 3.4%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，高於前月的 0.2%，但年增率由
2.5%降至 2.4%。在細項方面，能源價格由跌轉升，能源指數月增 2.1%，其中汽
油價格上漲 3.9%，成為推升整體物價的主要因素；住房指數月增 0.3%，食品指

數月增 0.1%。此外，通訊、外宿住宿、機票、教育及車輛價格均呈上漲，醫療照

2026 年 8 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 0.4%，高於上月之
0.1%，年增率則由上月的 4.8%升至 5.4%。其中最終需求商品價格月增 1.1%，結
束前兩個月下跌走勢 ， 超過四分之三的漲幅係由能源價格月增4.2%所帶動 ； 細項
中，柴油價格大漲 24.1%，汽油、航空燃油及燃料油價格亦同步上升，食品價格
則微增 0.1%。另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.1%，已連續第三個月上升，
主要受運輸及倉儲服務價格上漲 2.3%支撐 ， 其中貨車貨運服務及航空客運服務價
格分別上漲 2.0%及 4.2%，惟批發及零售業利潤率下滑，使部分服務價格漲幅受
到抵銷。此外，剔除食品、能源及貿易服務後之核心最終需求PPI 月增 0.3%，低
於 7 月之 0.4%，年增率維持 4.7%。整體而言，8 月 PPI 回升主要反映能源價格反
彈及運輸成本上升影響，顯示美國生產端通膨壓力仍具韌性；惟核心 PPI 增幅較
前月略為放緩，基礎通膨動能尚未進一步升溫，後續仍須持續關注能源價格波動
及服務成本變化對整體物價走勢之影響。 
近期美加貿易衝突持續升溫，雙方關稅報復行動已進一步擴大至非關稅措施
領域 。 加拿大自9 月 8 日起正式對約 200 億美元以上美國商品課徵 15%至 50%不
等的報復性關稅，涵蓋鋼鐵、鋁製品、家電、服飾及農業設備等產品，以回應美
國先前對加拿大商品加徵關稅措施 。 面對加拿大反制 ， 美國政府隨即宣布擴大限
制措施，除關稅措施外，將自 9 月 29 日起限制部分加拿大乳製品、酒類及機車
產品輸美，並限制加拿大企業參與部分美國政府採購標案，使雙方爭端由關稅戰
延伸至市場准入及政府採購領域。 加拿大總理 Mark Carney 表示，美國措施可
能衝擊加拿大關鍵產業發展，加方將持續採取對等回應措施。市場亦關注美方後
續可能進一步提高加拿大汽車、鋼鐵及相關零組件關稅，增添北美供應鏈及企業
投資規劃不確定性。整體而言，美加經貿關係短期內仍難見明顯緩和，後續發展
恐進一步增添北美及全球貿易前景不確定性。 
美國國會近期推進針對俄羅斯的新制裁法案，內容包括授權總統對俄羅斯原
油或天然氣主要購買國的進口商品課徵最高 100%關稅，以進一步限制俄羅斯能
源收入並加大對俄施壓 。 該法案已獲參議院以高票通過 ， 眾議院則預計展開審議 ，

二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期升息一碼，且仍有繼續升息的空間 
 歐元區第二季經濟成長季率終值由初值上修 0.2 個百分點至 0.6%，但上修主
因來自愛爾蘭經濟大幅成長 10.2%；若排除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第二季經濟成長季
率僅約 0.3%。從需求面觀察，第二季消費貢獻 0.2 個百分點，雖然通膨上升侵蝕
購買力，但薪資漲幅仍優於預期，加上高儲蓄率支持，使消費動能得以延續。其
中 ， 服務消費維持穩健成長 ， 耐久財支出亦明顯增加 。 另方面 ， 淨出口貢獻0.9 個
百分點，但設備投資下滑使整體固定投資表現仍顯疲弱。 
 歐洲央行 9 月 10 日如期宣布升息一碼，存款機制利率調升為 2.5%，但仍維
持「依經濟數據逐次決策」的立場。值得注意的是，本次歐洲央行分別上修2026
年、2027 年經濟成長率 0.1、0.2 個百分點至 0.9%、1.4%，以及 2028 年核心通膨
上修 0.1 個百分點至 2.3%， 反映歐洲央行認為經濟尚具韌性 ， 即便通膨壓力增溫 ，
仍能減輕其負面影響，使得政策利率繼續上調的空間，故一般預期歐洲央行將會
在 10 月政策會議採取觀望，並在12 月宣布升息一碼，2027 年第一季升息機率亦
有所上升，歐洲央行貨幣政策短期內仍將維持偏鷹立場。 
三、日本—內閣府上修第二季經濟成長，薪資動能續強 
2026 年第二季經濟成長率由 1.1%上修至 1.4%，主要受國內需求及庫存
貢獻改善帶動，其中以資本支出修正為國內需求上修的主因。展望下半年，
在政府財政刺激措施、企業持續擴大設備投資及薪資成長帶動消費支出的支
撐下，日本經濟可望維持溫和成長態勢，惟外部需求復甦力道、能源價格變
動及地緣政治風險仍為影響經濟表現的不確定因素。 
7 月薪資年增 4.7%，優於市場預期，其中夏季獎金年增 6.3%為主要推升
因素；經常性薪資及兼職時薪分別年增 4.1%及 5.7%，增速均較前期擴大 ， 顯
示薪資成長動能持續增強。此外，實質薪資年增3.4%，反映勞工購買力進一
步改善。在企業獲利表現穩健支撐下，儘管仍面臨成本上升及外部環境不確
定性 ， 企業仍維持獎金發放與加薪趨勢 ， 有助支撐民間消費韌性及經濟表現 。

四、中國—進出口同步強勁成長，貿易順差增幅則相對有限 
8 月出口年增率較 7 月回升 1.1 個百分點至 25%，出口總值為 4,014 億美元；
累計前八個月出口年增率為 19.3%，累積出口總值為 29,238 億美元。前八個月
IC、自動數據處理設備(包含伺服器等)累積出口額分別成長 103.9%、49.4%；船
舶累積出口成長 35.2%；汽車累積出口成長 53.2%。另方面，8 月進口年增率較 7
月回升 0.7 個百分點至 28.2%， 進口總值為2,824 億美元 ； 累計前八個月進口值成
長 28.2%至 21,183 億美元，其中 IC、自動數據處理設備(包含伺服器等)進口分別
成長 61.7%、105.1%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部分伺服器關鍵組件有賴
於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前八個月貿易順差達 8,055 億美元，僅較去年
同期增加 2.6%， 增幅明顯低於出口 ， 顯示出口擴張同時伴隨關鍵原物料進口大幅
增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價值產品依賴國際供應鏈支
持。 
8 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 7 月的 3.5%回升至 3.8%，主因原油價格自 7 月
起走升，其影響逐漸反映於上游生產成本。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格年
增率反彈 7.3 個百分點至 10.5%，並帶動燃料加工業通膨上揚，抵銷近期鋼鐵價
格下滑，導致相關礦產開採及冶煉加工產品價格較去年同期下滑的影響。此外，
對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學工業、化纖產業通膨沒有明顯變動，部分可能
與先前油價上漲期間累積較高庫存有關，惟在近期油價再度走升下，不排除相關
成本壓力於未來數月逐步反映。另因金價盤整，有色金屬礦採選業出廠價格年增
率亦持續放慢。 
8 月消費者通膨(CPI)回升 0.3 個百分點為 0.8%，但仍然是近七個月的次低水
準，核心通膨(不含食品及能源)回升 0.1 個百分點至 1 %。當月受國際原油價格上
漲影響，燃油通膨反彈 7.5 個百分點至 8.3%；手機零組件上漲，通信工具通膨上
揚 2.3 個百分點至 10.6%；夏季旅遊需求支持旅遊服務通膨增加 0.1 個百分點至
2.7%；其他商品及服務價格則大致平穩。整體而言，8 月能源價格有所回升，但
在企業庫存水位仍偏高情況下，抑制成本的傳導速度，除燃油價格外，尚未明顯
反映於其他消費項目通膨。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，若油價維持高檔或
進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。

五、印度及東南亞—油價上揚、通膨升溫，進口成本上漲恐使貿易收支惡化 
東南亞:(1)印尼：7 月出口額 262.2 億美元、年增 6.05%，非油氣產品出口年增
6.84%，礦物燃料、鋼鐵及電氣機械及設備出口增幅均逾 2 成，油氣產品出口
大幅下滑 14.58%；對美國（8.61%）、中國（17.52%）、日本（7.93%）、東
協（16.72%） 出口強勁成長。7 月進口年增 27.02%達 260.9 億美元，創歷史新
高，非油氣產品及油氣產品進口分別年增 23.83%、49.91%，以機械設備、電
機及電氣設備及塑膠品進口成長為主 ， 自中國、 歐盟進口成長23.49%、26.32%、
自美進口成長 13.1%。進出口相抵，7 月貿易收支意外轉為順差 1.3 億美元。
印尼經濟較依賴進口 ， 部分進口需求係配合國內投資及生產活動擴張 ，另國際
油價上漲增加能源進口負擔，在出口成長幅度相對有限情形下 ， 進口持續快速
增加將壓縮商品貿易順差，並可能進一步擴大經常帳赤字。8 月消費者物價指
數(CPI)年增率升至 3.19%，主要因食品、飲料和香菸價格上漲，以及個人照護
和交通成本增加所致 ，剔除波動較大的食品和政府管制價格的核心通膨率升至
2.92%，為 2023 年 3 月以來最高。儘管通膨有所回升，但仍維持在印尼央行
1.5%至 3.5%的目標區間內。(2)泰國：8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率上升至
2.53%，主要因中東衝突導致油價上漲，進而推高國內燃料價格所致，食品、
非食品價格通膨分別上升 2.99%、2.23%，剔除能源及生鮮食品的核心通膨率
上揚至 1.44%，創 3 年多新高，泰國商務部維持其 2026 年整體通膨預期在
1.5%-2.5%之間，通膨水準仍處於泰國央行 1%-3%的目標區間內。(3)菲律賓：
8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率從 7 月的 6.2%降至 6.1%， 主要因食品/非酒精
飲料、房屋/公用事業價格漲幅放緩，惟受燃油價格上漲影響，交通運輸價格
進一步上揚 13.5%，核心通膨率小幅降至 4.1%，1 月至 8 月平均通膨率為 5.2%，
仍高於菲律賓央行 2%-4%的目標區間，在國際油價大幅上揚下，央行升息壓
力漸增。(4)越南: 8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率上升至 4.89%，創 3 個月新
高，主要是運輸成本上升所致，儘管高於越南國家銀行(SBV)4.5%目標，惟核
心通膨穩定於 4.5%， 在經濟動能仍佳下 ， 央行尚有餘裕採取審慎觀望的政策。
8 月出口額年增 26%至 548 億美元，其中電子產品出口成長 41%為主要驅動
力，機械設備成長 34%居次，對美國出口大增 24.7%；8 月進口額年增 37.9%
至 549.1 億美元，以電子產品、電腦及零件成長 80%為最，機械設備居次。進
出口相抵，8 月貿易逆差收窄至 11 億美元。整體而言，越南受惠於人工智慧
需求及中國供應鏈移轉，帶動電子產品出口，但主要集中於外資企業，資本財

用相關內容。

9

與生產投入高度仰賴進口， 對國內生產活動增值十分有限 ，越南政府正積極推
動投資技術轉移、提升治理能力及開拓市場，加強本土企業之間的聯繫，以創
造更大的附加價值。 
五、台灣—通膨略降溫，AI 需求支撐出口成長 
8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.04%，雖較前兩月回落，但已連續 4 個月高
於 2%的通膨警戒線，主要受國際油價上漲帶動油料費、機票及旅遊團費增加，
加上外食費、房租及電費等價格上漲所致；惟蔬菜價格因去年基期較高而大幅下
跌，抵銷部分漲幅。整體而言，通膨壓力雖略有降溫，但核心 CPI 仍達 2.30%，
顯示外食、房租等服務類價格仍具黏著性，物價上漲壓力尚未完全消退。 
 8 月出口達 824 億美元，年增 41%，續創歷年單月新高，主要受惠於人工智
慧應用快速擴展及國際雲端服務業者持續加大資本支出，帶動半導體、伺服器及
相關資通訊產品需求強勁。雖然部分傳統產業仍受全球景氣復甦步調不一及貿易
環境不確定性影響，但科技產品需求熱絡使整體出口表現優於預期。展望未來，
在 AI、 高效能運算及雲端服務需求持續成長 ， 以及第四季傳統外銷旺季來臨帶動
下，我國出口動能可望延續，惟仍須關注地緣政治風險及國際貿易政策變化對外
需之影響。

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？ 
        ——2026 年9 月价格数据点评

事件：2026 年 9 月全国居民消费价格指数（CPI）同比 0.9%（前
值 0.8%），环比 0.2%（前值 0.4%），1-9 月 CPI 同比 0.9%（前值
0.9%）；核心 CPI 同比 1%（前值 1%），环比0（前值 0.1%），1-9 月
核心 CPI 同比 1.1%（前值 1.1%）；工业生产者出厂价格指数（PPI）
同比5%（前值3.8%），环比1.2%（前值0.4%），1-9 月PPI 同比2.3%
（前值2%）。 
受油价、芯片等涨价影响，CPI 环比连续两个月为正,9 月略高于
季节性水平，CPI 同比涨幅较上月小幅扩大；1-9 月，CPI 同比及核心
CPI 同比均较上年底 回升但仍低于上半年水平 ，价格持续修复 但修复
节奏放缓。9 月 CPI 同比增长 0.9%，较上月回升 0.1 个百分点，连续
两个月回升，环比延续正增长， 为 0.2%，强于季节性水平（2023 年-
2025 年，9 月 CPI 环比均值为 0.1%）。9 月核心 CPI（剔除食品和能
源）同比增长 1%，与上月持平，仍位于较低水平， 环比 0，持平季节
性水平（2023 年-2025 年，9 月核心CPI 环比均值为0）。1-9 月，CPI
同比及核心 CPI 同比分别为 0.9%和 1.1%，均与 1-8 月持平，较上年底
和上年同期水平回升，但均较上半年回落 0.1 个百分点，仍位于较低
区间水平，价格修复的基础有待进一步夯实。 
 油价仍是 CPI 环比维持正增的重要支撑， 同时受成本走高影响， 本
月移动电话机、计算机、数据存储设备和平板电脑 价格环比上涨、
对CPI 环比维持正增带来一定助力。工业消费品中，受国际市场价格
变动影响，9 月国内汽油价格环比上涨4.9%，连续两个月环比正增，
油价仍是CPI 环比维持正增的重要支撑。受芯片等成本上涨影响，从
7 月开始，多家手机厂商已经陆续上调部分在售产品价格。9 月 1 日，

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？ 
华为、小米、荣耀多款手机官方调整售价，单次涨幅从 200 元至 1000 元不等。9 月移动电话
机、计算机、数据存储设备和平板电脑价格环比分别上涨 3.7%、2.0%、1.7%和 1.5%，合计影
响 CPI 环比上涨约 0.04 个百分点。此外，秋装上市，带动服装价格环比上涨 0.7%，影响 CPI
环比上涨约0.03 个百分点。 
 暑期效应消退，服务价格环比由涨转降，持平季节性水平，对 CPI 环比的影响由上月支撑转
为拖累，其中飞机票、宾馆住宿等 假期出行相关服务价格环比均由正转负。 9 月，服务价格
同比上涨0.8%，涨幅与上月相同，服务价格环比由上月上涨0.1%转为下降0.2%，持平季节性
（2023-2025 年9 月，服务价格环比均值为-0.2%），对CPI 环比的影响由上月上拉约0.04 个
百分点转为下拉约 0.08 个百分点。具体来看，随着暑假结束，飞机票、宾馆住宿环比均由正
转负，飞机票、宾馆住宿和旅行社收费价格环比分别下降 8.7%、7.0%和 6.0%，合计影响 CPI
环比下降约0.12 个百分点。 
 食品价格环比维持正增长 ，但低于季节性水平 ，同比降幅边际扩大，食品价格仍偏弱 。9 月
食品价格同比下降 1.7%，降幅比上月扩大 0.3 个百分点，影响 CPI 同比下降约 0.29 个百分
点，环比上涨0.4%，连续两个月正增长，影响CPI 环比上涨约0.06 个百分点，但仍低于季节
性水平（2023-2025 年 9 月，食品价格环比均值为 0.6%）。其中，受猪肉供给结构调整以及
暑假、中秋和国庆备货需求带动，鲜菜及猪肉价格环比连续 3 个月为正。此外，休渔期结束，
水产品价格环比连续两个月为负。

煤炭、非金属矿物制品等部分行业价格上涨；人工智能快速发展，AI 产业链部分需求增加、
价格上涨，上述因素均对 PPI 形成支撑。从行业来看，受原油、有色金属等海外定价大宗商

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？ 
品价格上行影响，石油开采、精炼石油产品制造、有机化学原料制造价格环比分别上涨14.5%、
7.4%和5.4%，有色金属矿采选业、有色金属冶炼和压延加工业价格环比分别上涨1.6%和1.5%，
5 个行业合计影响PPI 环比上涨约0.63 个百分点，是本月PPI 环比改善最重要的支撑。同时，
受冬季供暖备煤需求增加、主产矿区供应阶段性偏紧 等因素影响，煤炭开采和洗选业、煤炭
加工价格环比分别上涨9.5%和13.6%。9 月是建筑施工旺季，钢材、水泥等建筑材料需求有所
回升，加之 煤炭等原材料成本上涨，非金属矿物制品业 （主要包括水泥、玻璃等） 价格环比
由负转正，上涨0.3%，黑色金属冶炼和压延加工业价格环比上涨0.2%，4 个行业合计影响PPI
环比上涨约 0.38 个百分点。此外，人工智能、算电协同等领域加快发展，电气机械和器材制
造业、计算机通信和其他电子设备制造业等 AI 产业链相关行业价格环比均上涨 0.4%，亦对
PPI 上行带来支撑。  
 生活资料价格环比连续两个月为正但仍位于低位区间，同比降幅收窄至 0 附近。生活资料价
格同比降幅连续 3 个月收窄但仍为负，9 月同比下降 0.1%，环比延续 8 月的正增长态势、连
续两个月为正，不过仍位于0 左右的低位区间，终端需求仍偏弱。

如何看待PPI-CPI 剪刀差走扩？

整体来看，今年以来物价整体呈现温和修复态势，呈现以下特征：一是上下游景气度存
在温差，中下游尤其下游中小企业仍在承受成本挤压 。今年以来，生产资料与生活资料同比
分化走势逐渐加大，二者剪刀差由年初 1 月的 0.4 个百分点扩大到 9 月的 6.6 个百分点。结
合 PMI 数据来看，截至 9 月，主要原材料购进价格分项与出厂价格剪刀差连续两个月扩大。
涨价能力和利润持续向上游资源行业集中，终端消费品价格整体仍偏弱，中下游制造业面临
成本挤压。二是 AI 产业链涨价正逐步成为影响物价走势的新增变量。从电气机械、算力设
施等上游环节，到存储器件、平板终端、手机等下游消费品，AI 相关产品价格表现均较好，
成为价格修复的重要力量。三是当前经济仍面临供给端涨价与需求端疲软并存的矛盾，PPI-
CPI 剪刀差扩大。自今年 4 月 PPI 同比首次超过 CPI 同比以来，PPI-CPI 正剪刀差由 4 月的
1.6%波动扩大至 9 月的 4.1%，9 月较 8 月扩大 1.1 个百分点。供需失衡的格局仍在延续，价
格修复仍受到掣肘。 
后续来看，9 月以来中东局势再度升级，国际原油价格波动上行，截至10 月13 日，WTI
原油期货结算价由 9 月底的 90.42 美元/桶，波动上行至 94.1 美元/桶。若油价持续高位运
行，短期石化产业链价格仍将位于较高水平， PPI 同比保持韧性仍有支撑，并沿产业链向
CPI 能源分项传导。但也要看到，当前生产端价格改善仍主要集中于能源、有色和部分新动
能行业，终端需求相关行业价格仍偏弱，核心CPI 始终在1%左右的较低水平徘徊，全面物价
上涨的可能性较低，完成2%的年度通胀目标难度仍较大。

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Powertech Technology
Subsidiaries drive stronger revenue/OP growth;
FOPLP expansion back on track
Q326 results broadly in-line despite better GM
Q326 EPS of NT$3.54 was broadly in-line with UBSe/consensus as stronger  revenue and
better GM were offset by higher tax and minority interests. Q326 sales rose 9.6% QoQ
to NT$25.3 bn on robust DRAM/eSSD demand and price hikes, ahead of UBSe/Street's
+5-6% QoQ.  GM expanded by 2.2ppt QoQ to 24.0%, supported by better pricing,

higher loadings and more favourable  mix. By business, higher GM testing  increased by
1ppt QoQ to 24% of total sales, while lower margin SiP/module declined 2ppt QoQ to
9%, and packaging  increased 1ppt QoQ to 67%. By entity, we estimate 43%-owned
Greatek  and 61%-owned Teraprobe  each delivered mid-teens % QoQ revenue growth
in Q326, versus  only mid-single % growth at PTI standalone. The stronger contribution
from subsidiaries, however, resulted in higher effective tax rate and minority interests.

Near-term outlook  remains constructive
Management remains constructive on the near-term outlook, supported by AI/cloud
investment cycle, continued memory supply constraint, and PTI's ongoing diversification
into non-memory segments. Q426F UTRs are expected to remain elevated following
over-90% levels in Q3, while ASPs should continue to increase as customers absorb
higher raw material costs. PTI is also expanding its exposure to logic Flip-Chip BGA, while

premium-segment automotive demand remains stable. Nevertheless, consumer
electronics demand is showing some weakness amid higher memory pricing. We
forecast Q426 sales  to grow 4% QoQ and  GM to expand  0.8ppt QoQ to 24.8%.
Looking into 2027E, we believe  GM to remain in the mid-20% range, as continued
memory supply tightness and  better mix should largely offset higher  depreciation.
FOPLP expansion back on track
PTI indicated its FOPLP expansion remains on track for mass production in 2027, with the

equipment delivery schedule having improved. It sees robust customer demand  with
notable increases in New Product Introduction (NPI). We continue to model FOPLP
capacity of 3K substrates/month in 2026E, rising to 6K by mid-2027E in Taiwan, with
panel substrate size of 510 mm x 515 mm. Importantly, PTI  aims to serve end-customers
directly and capture greater value-add, rather than operate  as an outsourcing partner to
leading wafer foundry. Its JV with Broadcom remains  under review by Taiwan's MOEA

Investment Commission with management expecting a decision in the  near-term.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$280, retain NEUTRAL
We revise our 2026/27/28E EPS by 3%/9%/7% on better sales/GM, offset by higher tax
and minority interest from subsidiaries. We raise PT to NT$280 (from NT$260) with the
same 17x 2027E PE. While near-term outlook has improved we believe further upside
will depend on the execution of the  FOPLP ramp and the sustainability of pricing

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Powertech: We value Powertech using PE methodology.
Upside/downside risks: 1) A stronger/weaker pricing environment; 2) slower-than expected
tech end demand and memory market recovery; and 3) faster/slower ramp of FOPLP and CPO
businesses.

the 12-month timeframe reflected in any equity rating set out in this note.  For previous responses please make reference to (i) previous UBS

Team on ubs-quant-answers@ubs.com. A consolidated report which contains all responses is also available and again you should contact your

UBS sales representative for details and pricing or the Quantitative Research Team on the email above.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over the next 12
months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA)  is the one-year local
market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR) Stocks may be flagged
by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.
EXCEPTIONS AND SPECIAL CASES:UK and European Investment Fund ratings and definitions are: Buy:  Positive on factors
such as structure, management, performance record and discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record and discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record and discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
6426 統新 
(Apogee Optocom Co.)

報告日期： 2026/10/19 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要： 
 統新 2Q26 營收為 2.08 億元，+34.7%QoQ、+82.4%YoY，毛利率
躍升至 56.20%，稅後淨利 0.86 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.22 元。
2Q26 受惠生產效率提升、產能滿載毛利率躍升。 
 AI 伺服器帶動統新的 DWDM 產品延伸至 AI 資料中心 DCI 主要
骨幹濾片，光纖波段分離與光纖傳輸頻寬增加，會需要統新薄
膜濾光片作為訊號傳輸強化。除 Scale-Across 長距離骨幹，分
散式 AI 數據中心，專門做長距離光通訊傳輸 DWDM 市場，
2026~2031 年每年維持 31%年複合成長。 
 1.6T 光纖收發模組 ， 未來 2 年呈倍數增長 。 1.6T 光纖收發模組，
在後端 PD(接收端)改採 200G PD，會用到 8 片統新之薄膜濾光
片。

一. 統新 2Q26 營收與財報優於市場預期，3Q26 續強 
統新 2Q26 營收為 2.08 億元，+34.7%QoQ、+82.4%YoY，毛利率躍
升至 56.20%，稅後淨利 0.86 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.22 元。主要受
惠於 AI 資料中心互連需求，使得統新產線維持滿載。展望 3Q26
預估統新營收為 2.42 億元，+16.35%QoQ，毛利率 56.58%，稅後淨
利 1.06 億元，單季稅後 EPS+2.76 元。統新預計產能增加 30%，以
因應客戶需求。

二. 2026 年 DWDM 隨 AI 骨幹網絡建置，濾片量增價漲 
統新高密度波長多工器( D W D M，D e n s e  W a v e l e n g t h  D i v i s i o n  
Multiplexing)，至少可傳輸 16 種不同顏色的光纖，最高可傳輸 128
種不同顏色之光纖，同一條主幹網路上，可以傳輸的頻寬因此加
大，相當具有傳輸效率。DWDM 於發送端將不同波長的光信號組
合起來(覆用, MUX)，並耦合到光纜線路上的同一根光纖中，進行
傳輸，在接收端又將多個波長的光信號分開(解覆用, DEMUX)，並
作進一步處理，恢復出原波長信號後送入不同的終端接收器。統
新的 DWDM 產品延伸至 AI 資料中心 DCI 主要骨幹濾片，波段分
離 ( S -B a n d、C - B a n d 傳 輸 核 心、L -B a nd 容 量 擴 充、S -B a nd 未 來 預 留、
U-Band 8nm 用於 OTDR 光纖監控偵測主幹網路是否具有斷點或需
要救援)，統新以單通道 100GHz 去細分 DWDM 濾光片，統新推出
新品，50GHz 間隔濾光片，支援的通道數量提升至 96Channels。 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：375.00 元

三.  AI 伺服器高速傳輸，帶動光通訊主被動元件量增 
Scale-Across 長距離骨幹，分散式 AI 數據中心，專門做長距離光
通訊傳輸 DWDM 市場，2026~2031 年，DWDM 市場每年維持 31%
年 複合 成長。此 外，受惠 AI 伺服 器傳 輸速 率提升，如：Broadcom、
Marvell 等國際晶片廠商，於 2026 年起已推出 800G /1.6T Switch 晶
片組，等同於 AI 伺服器後端 Ports 支援的光纖通訊傳輸會一併升
級至 800G 與 1.6T 傳輸速率。Yole 報告指出，1.6T 光纖收發模組
出貨量，2026 年/2027 年/2028 年預估量 15~20M/55~60M/85~100M，
1.6T 光纖收發模組 ， 未來 2 年內呈倍數增長 。 1.6T 光纖收發模組，
在後端 PD(接收端)改採 200G PD ， 會用到 8 片統新之薄膜濾光片。
在薄膜濾光片鍍膜層數 ， Scale-Across 與 NPO 所需薄膜濾光片層數
約 150L~200L，未來發展至 CPO 時鍍膜層數會再增加 8L，統新為
此預計多準備 10 條產線預計開發 250L+規格，予台系知名晶圓代
工廠商。統新指出，Scale-Out 濾片鍍膜代工量增加，是替中系系
統廠代工(層數約 100~150L)。Scale-Across 濾片鍍膜層數會大於
200L，甚至高達 250L，目前訂單能見度高，涵蓋 1.)同業產能難以
支應，Cohr 交給統新、2.)中系系統廠商加單、3.)台系網通廠商於
低軌衛星鍍膜(LEO 非光學鍍膜) 。統新毛利率之所以跳升，是先前
客戶給統新的試產量僅 20~30 片，2Q26 起量產量增至 K 級別，出
貨量增加、規模經濟效應之下，單月營收損平點(評估為 0.4~0.5
億元)，2Q26 起月營收顯著跳升至 0.6~0.7 億元。預期此營運動能
至少能見度到 4Q26，但由於 DWDM 交期延長至 6~8 個月，預估
2027 年產業供需偏向緊俏。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 375 元 
統新競爭優勢在於其生產高品質高效率之薄膜濾光片篩選特定輻
射波段的光學元件，能篩選光的強度、改變光譜成分或限定震動
截面，藉由 CWDM/DWDM 技術使相同數據集中於同一根光纖中 
傳輸，增大傳輸容量進而提高光纖的使用效率，生產良率逐步提
升且具佔有一定之市場地位。預估統新 2026 年營收 8.56 億元，
+75.77%YoY，毛利率 53.43%，稅後淨利 3.32 億元，稅後 EPS 為
+8.63 元。展望 2027 年，隨產能積極擴充之下，預估統新年營收
為 17.02 億元，+98.83%YoY ，毛利率預估為 54.85% ，稅後淨利 5.57
億元，稅後 EPS+14.47 元。考量基礎建設帶動 DWDM 規格升級，
AI 伺服器傳輸速率提升因應 1.6T 傳輸薄膜濾光片用量同步增長，
給予統新投資建議買進，目標價 375 元(26.00 X2027 EPS)。

一.公司簡介

統新(6426)為生產精密光學元件，以及高密度分波多工器(DWDM)
關鍵元件─薄膜濾光片(TFF)之專業廠商。各產品線營收占比為主
動光學元件佔比低於 15%、被動光學元件：80~85%、光學鍍膜元
件與代工低於 10%。主要產品 DWDM 濾光片 (Dense Wavelength 
Division Multiplexer，即為高密度波長多工分工器)技術可以大幅提
高光纖通訊系統之頻寬，成為光纖通訊系統的市場中關注的焦
點。統新國內主要競爭對手為東典光電科技(股)，國外廠商包含
II-IV Cohernet(美國)、Iridian Technologies(加拿大)等廠商投入相關
產品研發及生產。

圖一、光通訊元件

二.26 年營運概況及 26 年展望 
1.統新 DWDM 技術層層堆疊良率優異  
統新主要產品為薄膜濾光片即為篩選特定輻射波段的光學元件，
能篩選光的強度、改變光譜成分或限定震動截面，藉由不同光線
原理而選擇通過的光源(紅、藍、綠光) ，且藉由 CWDM/DWDM 技
術使相同數據集中於同一根光纖中傳輸，增大傳輸容量進而提高
光纖的使用效率。

2.因應 AI 資料中心基建商機，薄膜濾光片產品走向更高規  
回顧 5G 時期的薄膜濾光片，支援頻段增加，切割更細，中心波

## Document 198: 9月工业企业利润数据点评：利润增速反弹，价格驱动增强

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月工业企业利润数据点评：利润增速反弹，价格驱动增强.pdf
Report source: 联储证券
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告 
宏观经济点评 2026 年 10 月 28 日

请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明

9 月工业企业利润数据点评：利润增速反弹，价格
驱动增强

[Table_Author] 
 魏争 分析师  沈夏宜 分析师

Email:weizheng@lczq.com Email:shenxiayi@lczq.com  
证书:S1320524100001 证书:S1320523020004

### Page 2

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
6.7%，PPI 同比下降 2.0%；累计营业收入增长 2.6%，低于营业成本
3.8%的增速，利润同比下降 22.8%，降幅扩大 6.8 个百分点。二是酒饮等
部分消费行业利润边际改善，但需求修复仍不稳固。 酒、饮料和精制茶制
造业利润同比降幅由 34.7%明显收窄至 14.3%；医药制造业利润增速由
3.9%升至 4.4%；家具制造业利润降幅由 51.8%收窄至 51.3%；食品制造
业利润由增长 1.6%转为下降 0.5%。 
名义库存继续回升，实际补库动能并未同步增强，利润改善向经营现金流
的传导有待观察。从库存端看， 9 月末规上工业企业产成品存货同比增
长 11.6%，较 8 月提高 0.6 个百分点；按 PPI 粗略调整后的库存增速约为
6.6%，较 8 月回落 0.6 个百分点，指向名义库存加快增长部分受到价格上
涨影响，尚不能据此判断企业主动补库意愿增强。从经营效率看，库存及
回款周转效率边际改善，但经营资金占用压力仍存。9 月，产成品存货周
转天数由 21.3 天缩短至 21.0 天，应收账款平均回收期由 72.2 天缩短至
71.6 天；但应收账款同比增速由 9.0%升至 10.0%，企业负债同比增速由
7.0%升至 7.4%，指向周转效率改善尚未伴随应收账款及负债增长放缓，
利润改善能否进一步转化为现金流改善和主动扩张 仍有待观察。 
利润反弹主要受价格和利润率改善驱动，后续持续性仍取决于内需能否有
效接力。短期看，能源价格和 AI 产业链仍是工业利润的两条主要支撑，
但持续性存在差异。一方面，能源链盈利受国际大宗商品价格波动影响较
大；另一方面，AI 产业链盈利更多取决于订单和资本开支景气的延续。9
月 PPI 与工业利润同步反弹，但营收增速并未明显加快，指向盈利改善尚
未形成需求、收入与利润的良性循环。若后续上游价格持续上涨，而中下
游产品价格和订单未能同步改善，部分传统制造业仍将面临成本压力。 往
后看，重点关注三个信号，一是需求改善能否推动价格修复由上游向加工
工业及终端消费品扩散；二是新型政策性金融工具、 “六张网”等重大项
目能否加快形成实物工作量，带动传统工业品需求和收入修复； 三是应收
账款回收和库存周转效率能否持续改善，推动利润修复向经营现金流和主
动投资传导。

风险提示：国际大宗商品价格波动超预期；AI 产业链需求及外需景气回落
超预期；内需改善和政策落地进度不及预期。

投资评级说明 
投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价 （或行业
指数） 相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。 其中A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准； 新三板市场以三板成指 （针
对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

## Document 218: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% Y oY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% Y oY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다. 
목표주가 (유지) 2,000,000원

## Document 251: Anthropic能拿下“股市最大IPO”，却未必能有“债市投资级”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic能拿下“股市最大IPO”，却未必能有“债市投资级”.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

招股⽂件显⽰，公司已背负 62 亿美元流动债务，未启动租赁承诺更从 500 亿美元暴增⾄
3210 亿美元，还潜藏 770 亿美元额外借款选项。其依托博通构建的表外 " 芯⽚融资 " 结构，
更被视为循环交易对⼿⻛险隐患。对⽐ SpaceX 明确承诺投资级并顺利完成 250 亿美元债券
发⾏， Anthropic 对投资级信⽤评级⼏乎只字未提态度令债市买家难以定价。
摘要：
Anthropic 在备受瞩⽬的 IPO 招股⽂件中，对外界最关⼼的投资级信⽤评级问题⼏乎只字未提，
却同时警告可能⽐预期更早寻求新⼀轮融资。这⼀组合信号，正在让债券市场的潜在买家陷⼊
观望。
招股⽂件显⽰，截⾄今年 6 ⽉ 30 ⽇， Anthropic 已有 62 亿美元的流动债务，全部以可转换本票形
式⽋于亚⻢逊。与此同时，公司还披露了⼀笔由摩根⼠丹利牵头、规模⾼达 150 亿美元的五年期
循环信贷额度，以及从亚⻢逊、 Broadcom 和⾕歌⺟公司 Alphabet 处再借 770 亿美元可转换票据
的潜在选项。更令市场瞩⽬的是，截⾄ 6 ⽉底， Anthropic 尚未启动的租赁承诺 —— 主要与算⼒和

基础设施容量相关 —— 已从去年 12 ⽉底的约 500 亿美元骤升⾄ 3210 亿美元。
债务规模的急剧膨胀，使投资级评级的重要性愈发凸显。⼀旦获得投资级评级， Anthropic 将能
以更低成本进⼊保险公司、养⽼基⾦等⼤型机构投资者主导的⾼评级债券市场，从⽽打开更深
厚的资本池。然⽽，穆迪、标普和惠誉⽬前均尚未发布对 Anthropic 的评级。
招股⽂件对投资级评级态度暧昧
与同为科技独⻆兽的 SpaceX 相⽐， Anthropic 在投资级评级问题上的表态明显更为保守。据彭博
报道， SpaceX 在今年早些时候准备上市时，在招股⽂件中明确表⽰ " 致⼒于维持投资级信⽤评
级 " ，并随即获得穆迪、标普和惠誉三家机构的投资级认定，随后在 6 ⽉完成了⼀笔 250 亿美元的
投资级债券交易。
Anthropic 的招股⽂件则并未如此清晰地勾勒出投资级路径。这⼀态度上的差异，令债券市场潜
在买家难以形成明确预期，也使外界对其信⽤质量的判断更加复杂。
尽管措辞审慎，招股⽂件中的部分条款仍透露出 Anthropic 对投资级评级的隐性诉求。

那笔 150 亿美元的循环信贷额度 —— 由摩根⼠丹利牵头，于 9 ⽉完成，规模较此前约 30 亿美元⼤
幅扩容 —— 包含⼀项关键条款：若 Anthropic 获得⾄少两家主要评级机构的投资级认定，相关担
保和抵押权益将予以解除。
此外，该信贷额度的利率将随评级⾼低浮动： A 级评级对应基准利率上浮 75 个基点，⽽最低投资
级 BBB- 则对应上浮 125 个基点。这意味着，评级越⾼， Anthropic 的融资成本越低，激励机制清
晰可⻅。
资产负债表：流动性充裕，但承诺规模惊⼈
据报道，市场对 Anthropic 信⽤状况的判断，核⼼⽭盾在于：当前杠杆率尚在可控范围，但未来
承诺规模已令⼈咋⾆。
Impax Asset Management Group 投资级组合经理 Tony Trzcinka 表⽰：
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
70 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
这⼀判断精准点出了 Anthropic 信⽤故事的核⼼张⼒： 62 亿美元的现有债务尚不⾜为虑，但 3210
亿美元的未启动租赁承诺、以及潜在的 770 亿美元额外借款选项，构成了评级机构难以回避的重
⼤不确定性。
博通 “ 芯⽚融资 ” 模式：表外杠杆的隐忧
报道称，为应对快速扩张模型所需的巨额算⼒⽀出， Anthropic 采⽤了⼀种特殊的融资结构：依
托博通作为背书⽅，由专项企业实体发⾏债务购买芯⽚，再将芯⽚租赁给 Anthropic 使⽤。
⾸笔此类交易 “Big Sky” 规模达 350 亿美元，其中⼤部分由 Broadcom 提供⽀持，并获得了投资级
评级。本周⼀，博通宣布启动另⼀笔规模 630 亿美元的类似交易，⽤于为 Anthropic 采购芯⽚。

这⼀模式在⼀定程度上将债务压⼒转移⾄表外，但也形成了 Tony Trzcinka 所指出的 “ 循环交易对
⼿关系 ”—— 博通既是芯⽚供应商，⼜是融资结构的核⼼背书⽅，这种⾼度依赖单⼀对⼿⽅的安
排，可能成为评级机构审视的重点。
对于债券投资者⽽⾔，当前最直接的问题是：在穆迪、标普和惠誉正式发布评级之前，
Anthropic 的债券市场之路仍是⼀张⽩纸。
SpaceX 的先例提供了⼀个参照，但也带来了警⽰ ——SpaceX 6 ⽉完成的 250 亿美元投资级债券
交易发⾏后即遭抛售，债券价格持续下跌，令债券交易员深感震动。这⼀前⻋之鉴，或将使潜
在买家在 Anthropic 正式亮相债市时更加审慎。
随着未启动租赁承诺从 500 亿美元飙升⾄ 3210 亿美元， Anthropic 的融资需求只会持续扩⼤。能
否以投资级⾝份进⼊债市，将在很⼤程度上决定这家 AI 独⻆兽能否以可持续的成本⽀撑其雄⼼
勃勃的扩张计划。⽽这⼀答案，⽬前仍悬⽽未决。
“Anthropic ⽬前的状态是流动性⾮常充裕，但未来义务规模极为庞⼤。以当前杠杆⽔平⽽⾔，资

产负债表⽀持投资级评级。然⽽，最终评级将取决于评级机构如何看待承诺付款计划、较短的运
营历史，以及循环的交易对⼿关系。 ”

## Document 257: Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions #confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:15 October 13 2026 Share
Confidence posts its sharpest fall since the start of the Middle East conflict as elevated fuel
prices and higher interest rates weigh on business sentiment, while conditions hold steady
at weak levels. Conditions unchanged at -1 / Confidence -4pts to -13.

### Page 2

The September NAB Business Survey was in the field from 23 to 30 September, capturing ongoing
global uncertainty, still-rising fuel costs and, at least in part, the RBA’s September rate hike. Business
confidence continued to deteriorate, recording its sharpest fall since March and the onset of the Middle
East conflict. The index declined 4pts to -13 and, while remaining above the lows recorded earlier this
year, is now at its weakest level since May. At 18pts below its long-run average, the result underscores

the deep pessimism that continues to weigh on businesses. This broadly mirrors the pessimism evident
in the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index, where elevated fuel prices and higher
interest rates have coalesced in a similarly weak assessment of the outlook.
The headline business conditions index held steady in negative territory at -1, albeit rising 0.25pts on
an unrounded basis. While encouraging that conditions have not deteriorated further, the index has

now remained at -1 (rounded) for two consecutive months, its weakest level since the height of the
COVID-19 pandemic in 2020. At 7pts below its long-run average, the result points to continued
pressure across the business sector.
Across the major sub-components, employment moved into negative territory, falling 3pts to -1 and
sitting 4pts below its long-run average. This marks the first outright negative reading since November
2020 and provides further evidence of the gradual easing in labour market conditions. Against the

backdrop of compressed margins, the result may also indicate firms are becoming increasingly
cautious around hiring decisions. By contrast, trading conditions rose 1pt to 4, although they remain
8pts below their long-run average. The relative resilience in trading conditions is broadly consistent with

### Page 3

Westpac DataX Card-Tracker data, which suggests nominal spending remained resilient, albeit
slowing, through the September quarter despite ongoing headwinds facing consumers. Profitability
recorded the strongest improvement across the major components, rising 3pts to -5, although the sub-
index remains deeply subdued at 10pts below its long-run average.
Capacity utilisation edged 0.2ppt lower to 82.3%. While the monthly decline provides a tentative signal

of easing capacity pressures, utilisation remains 0.9ppt above its long-run average and continues to
trend higher on a three-month average basis. Capital expenditure continued to recover from its mid-
year weakness, rising 3pts to 7. While still below the highs recorded in the first quarter, the sub-index is
now trending higher on a three-month average basis, pointing to some stabilisation in investment
intentions.
Cost growth was comparatively subdued in September, although purchase cost pressures remain

elevated. Purchase cost growth edged 0.1ppt higher to 2.4%qtr, sitting 1.1ppt above its long-run
average and continuing to weigh on margins. By contrast, labour cost growth eased 0.1ppt to 1.7%qtr,
although it remains 0.5ppt above its long-run average. Final product price growth was unchanged at
0.8%qtr, 0.2ppt above its long-run average. The widening gap between purchase cost and final product
price growth suggests firms continue to absorb a meaningful share of higher input costs, consistent

with profitability remaining deeply below average. Looking ahead, the October survey will provide an
early indication of how the card surcharging ban is affecting business costs and the extent to which
these are passed through to final prices.

### Page 4

Related articles
Among other indicators, forward orders declined 1pt to -1, pointing to continued caution around the
demand outlook. Inventories, by contrast, rose 4pts (rounded) to 7, some 5pts above their long-run
average. In combination with weak forward orders, the inventory build points to a degree of slack
emerging across the business sector.

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 303: Federal Reserve preview_ A pause before a December hike

Source PDF: all report pdf/Federal Reserve preview_ A pause before a December hike.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Despite hot inﬂation prints in September and the Fed having
signalled that further action to tame inﬂation will likely be
needed, markets and economists are united in expecting the
central bank to leave interest rates on hold at the 28 October
FOMC meeting. Ongoing concerns about elevated price
pressures mean we do expect a December interest rate rise

James Knightley, Padhraic Garvey, CFA and Chris Turner
The Fed looks set to hold rates steady at the October FOMC meeting before
hiking again in December
Fed ofﬁcials play for time
The immediate reaction to the surprise September 0.3% month-on-
month core CPI print was to send the chances of an October rate
hike above 50%. But this didn’t last long. Earlier comments from
Federal Reserve Vice Chairs Philip Jeﬀerson and Michelle Bowman
and Governor Chris Waller, signalling no rush to decide on the next
policy move, quickly calmed markets. The fact that Fed Chair Kevin
Warsh subsequently chose not to talk up the chances of action
when he spoke at the IMF meetings in Bangkok, unlike at the

### Page 2

Jackson Hole Conference before the September hike, further
dampened the perceived chances of action.
While it is true to say that recent jobs numbers have been
disappointing and interest rate-sensitive sectors, such as the
housing market, are experiencing increasing pain, the economy is
growing robustly, and inﬂation remains above target. Fed oﬃcials
themselves have made it clear that they think more tightening of
monetary policy is going to be required to bring inﬂation to heel.

Given that the latest Federal Reserve Summary of Economic
Projections showed four FOMC members projecting 50bp of rate
hikes between now and the end of the year, the decision is unlikely
to be unanimous. Beth Hammack, Neel Kashkari and Lorie Logan
all voted to hike in July when the rest of the committee wanted to
hold steady. All three could again dissent in favour of hiking.
The press release is likely to be brief, and we also expect another
short and snappy press conference from Chair Warsh. He's likely to

keep things quite matter-of-fact given his lack of enthusiasm for
forward guidance. We expect his message to be that the economy
remains in decent shape, unemployment is low, and inﬂation is
above target. The policy changes being made will be determined
by the trends in incoming data, with oﬃcials currently thinking that
they probably have more work to do.

versus the Fed funds policy rate

Looking towards December
We will have two jobs reports and one inﬂation print ahead of the
December decision. The November CPI will be released the day after
the Fed meeting, and oﬃcials will almost certainly get to see it. As
such, they will have more information than the market does,

meaning a surprise can’t be completely ruled out.
We are forecasting a 25bp rate hike at the December meeting
(leaving the Fed funds target rate range at 4-4.25%) followed by
a prolonged pause through to December 2027, when we have that
December 2026 hike being reversed. This is in line with the current
consensus forecast, while the Fed’s own projections (its central
forecast) see one hike this year with rates left unchanged
throughout 2027.
While the sharp move higher in Treasury yields and the ﬁrmer

dollar are tightening ﬁnancial conditions to a degree, our forecast
path for the Fed assumes that in the couple of months following
the midterm elections we see an agreement that improves the ﬂow
of energy from the Persian Gulf, but signiﬁcantly, also allows for a
sharp reduction in shipping and insurance costs. It's the latter point
that is crucial to delivering lower pump prices at a time when
reﬁneries are working at capacity. If correct, this should

meaningfully ease inﬂation pressures, and with US wage growth
(the biggest cost input for corporate America) looking very benign
at just 3% and the largest component of the inﬂation basket –
housing at 35% by weight – set to experience more disinﬂation, we

### Page 4

believe overall US inﬂation can fall back to 2% in the second half of
2027.
The risk to our view is clearly tilted towards a longer-lasting conﬂict
rather than one that is resolved imminently. This means we would
not rule out additional hike(s) in 1Q 2027, but with households
experiencing a greater squeeze on spending power (weak wage
growth amid rising energy costs) and bond yields likely pushing
even higher – leading to new cycle highs for mortgage rates and

corporate borrowing costs – the risks of a weaker economy will
increase. As such, in this more stressed economic scenario,
eventual cuts to policy rates will likely be swifter and more
substantial in 2H 2027 than in our base case.
The Fed to leave well enough alone and let
Treasuries do their own thing
A solid performance for US Treasuries in the past week has acted to
reduce the prior tightening impulse that had gripped the market.
The 10yr yield below 5% obviously contrasts dramatically with the

prior break above 5.25%. We’re not convinced the more recent
downward lurch is persisting. But its genesis lends itself to a softer
approach from the FOMC at this meeting.
The risk-oﬀ tone creates a heightened risk proﬁle for the US
economy, raising the probability that the Fed holds at this meeting.
The battering that the French bond market has been taking is
another important impulse, as it heightens the broader sense of
bond angst. And at the margin, it can add to systemic pressure.

This is not the type of environment where the Fed would be inclined
to add to concerns by tightening policy. It’s the perfect meeting to
keep rates on hold.
Whether we remain below 5% for the 10yr yield is a whole other
question. We're minded to expect it to snap back above, as we still
have inﬂation, ﬁscal deﬁcit, and AI-boom impulses at play. The Fed
will no doubt remind us of these ongoing pressures. Plus, a break
back above 5% will likely need a degree of calm in the risk space

ﬁrst. We think it should ultimately move back above 5%; the timing,

We’re likely still some way from the Fed spelling out a plan to shrink

Chair Warsh. We expect recommendations from the various
committees to surface closer to the turn of the year. Meanwhile,
liquidity circumstances are stable, with repo behaving far better
than it did a year ago.

Warsh FOMC meetings have set the dollar
tone since June
The dollar should be able to hold on to most of its September
gains, as Fed communication is likely to remain supportive of
higher US yields. If the dollar is to correct lower, a renewed tech-led
risk-oﬀ move rather than a dovish shift in Fed messaging is the
more likely catalyst.
After a sharp 4% run higher through September and into early
October, the dollar has entered a more consolidative phase. Rate

diﬀerentials are the most important driver at the moment, and
these have moved slightly against the dollar this month as the US
tech sell-oﬀ has pared back Fed tightening expectations.
Broadening out the lens, it looks like Warsh-led FOMC meetings
have set the four-to-six-week trend for the dollar this year. June’s
meeting was hawkish, July’s was seen as dovish, and the Fed
delivered a hawkish hike in September.
We suspect that Warsh has learned from the criticism he received

over July's FOMC meeting, where he implied that the bond market
was doing the tightening (read as the Fed would not have to). That
probably means he will not be drawn into concluding that wider
credit spreads and softer equity prices mean tighter ﬁnancial
conditions now require fewer Fed hikes.
Assuming that Warsh sticks to the script that monetary policy
credibility remains the Fed’s top priority, short-dated US yields can
remain well-supported, and the dollar can largely hold on to the

gains it has made since early September. And the euro looks like it

## Document 320: Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Strategy Views_ Competition for Capital.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1. There are two themes dominating our investor conversations: the impact of AI and 
the rise in interest rates. The two issues are linked as demand for capital from both 
the private and public sectors increase. In the private sector a surge in capex 
spending to fund AI infrastructure has eaten into free cash flow and forced 
companies to raise more in debt and equity markets. Meanwhile, government 
borrowing needs have increased as priorities shift towards upgrading critical 
infrastructure, energy security and defense at a time when cyclical inflationary 
pressures driven by higher energy prices are also resulting in higher policy rates. The 
combination has pushed up the cost of capital. As recently as 2022, for example, 
30-year bond yields in Germany and Japan were close to zero (Exhibit 1). A rise in 
yields, together with more uncertainty (over geopolitics and the future impact of AI) 
have, in combination, pushed up the cost of capital.

2. This rise in yields comes after a near-record period of equity outperformance 
relative to bonds over 10-year holding periods (Exhibit 2).

Exhibit 1: Rising yields and uncertainty have increased the cost of capital 
30-year government bond benchmark yields (%)

### Page 2

As equities have continued to outperform bonds, equity risk premia have fallen back to 
levels last seen in the late 1990s, leaving equity markets more vulnerable to further 
increases in bond yields. That said, in the US and Japan, the ERP has bounced off recent 
lows (Exhibit 3).

3. The impact of rising bond yields, however, depends not only on the level of yields, but 
also on the reasons as well as the speed of the adjustment (Exhibit 4). It is the speed 
issue that has caused the biggest impact in the recent past. As our US strategists note, 
stocks have usually generated positive returns alongside rising interest rates unless the 
pace of rising rates exceeded 2+ standard deviations and this is a pattern that is broadly

reflected in other regions too. Today, a 2-standard deviation move in 10-year Treasury

the adjustments in recent days which explains the pullback in equity prices.

4. But despite the rise in yields, nominal GDP and profit growth remain strong and, until 
recently, the impact of rising bond yields has been offset by the strength in corporate 
profits. Earnings growth has been the main driver of equity returns over the past 18 
months in all regions (Exhibit 5). This strength has meant that PE multiples have either 
been flat (Japan and Europe) or have fallen (the US, Asia and EM).

the start of the year to 19x, in line with its long-run average, despite the market being 
close to its all-time high. 
5. While US equities have driven the bulk of equity outperformance for much of the 
fifteen years post the financial crisis, the improvement in earnings across regions, and 
the sharper de-rating in the US, has resulted in equity performance being 
broader-based by geography. Since 2025, the US equity market has been the weakest of 
the major regions (Exhibit 6), a turnaround from the dominant trend since the financial 
crisis and increasing investor returns from a more geographically diversified portfolio.

6. Supportive profit growth has been accompanied by positive earnings revisions, with 
2026 and 2027 estimates moving higher across major regions. There are four broad 
areas which have been driving much of this profit growth. First, technology earnings 
continue to be very robust. Second, rising energy prices have pushed up profits in the 
commodity sector. Third, banks have generally enjoyed strong earnings backed by 
positive nominal GDP growth, steep yield curves and strong private sector balance 
sheets. Finally, sectors such as Industrials have benefited from the surge in AI capex 
spending which has spilled over into improved revenues for the ‘pick and shovels’ of AI 
infrastructure. The breadth of the earnings’ growth has also increased the opportunity 
for investors to diversify across sectors as well as countries.

7. While technology profit growth has remained strong, the surge in capex spending 
among the hyperscalers has increasingly eaten through their free cash flow (Exhibit 8) 
prompting companies to look for alternative sources of funding, in the credit and equity 
markets.  The capex spending growth for AA-rated issuers has been substantial: the 65%

capex growth exceeds 35%. They have also turned to the convertible bond market 
where volume has also increased year to date reaching $135 billion in the US, with 
AI-related borrowers driving 44% of total issuance, on our estimates. 
Our Credit team raised their full-year US IG gross issuance forecast by $200bn to $2.3 
trillion and AI-related issuers account for a quarter of USD IG gross supply this year, 
pushing overall supply to a year-to-date record.

surge. Globally technology has seen its PE ratio moderate, falling below its 20-year 
median (Exhibit 10). Meanwhile, the spending of these companies has spilled over into 
better earnings and more visible cash flows in many other parts of the economy. 
Healthcare, Consumer Discretionary and Industrials are all more expensive in absolute 
terms than IT, and Industrials has re-rated and now has a PE close to the 90th percentile 
of its 20-year range.

9. For these reasons, we have argued that Technology equities have not experienced a 
classic bubble because valuation levels are much lower than in previous financial 
bubbles. Nevertheless, the risk for investors is less about valuation and more whether 
there is an unsustainable earnings bubble. Bubbles in earnings, rather than valuations, 
have occurred in previous periods.  The Bank sector, for example, briefly became the 
biggest sector in the S&P 500 in the run up to the financial crisis of 2008/09 (Exhibit 11). 
Unlike the technology bubble of the late 1990s, or the Japanese bubble of the late 
1980s, Bank stocks did not experience a major valuation bubble at the time, but the 
surge in earnings that drove the sector’s outperformance proved to be unsustainable.

### Page 8

There are, however, important differences to the Tech sector today. The earnings of the 
banks were boosted by sharply rising leverage and the asset that they were financing – 
US real estate – did experience a valuation bubble. When the housing market collapsed, 
pushing economies into recession, it triggered a sharp fall in underlying earnings for the 
banking sector. There are important differences today. First, technology profits remain 
very robust, and balance sheets are strong overall. Second, interest coverage ratios for 
the aggregate S&P 500 and its median stock, for example, rank in the 99th and 68th 
percentiles relative to the past 20 years. Third, demand for compute is accelerating and 
outstripping supply; Microsoft recently declared their intent to triple data centre 
capacity in 6 years. The bottlenecks are increasingly power, land and data centre 
capacity rather than a lack of compute demand. At our annual Communacopia 
Technology Conference, Nvidia reiterated its 2030 AI TAM outlook to $3tn-$4tn and 
supply constraints continue to be broad based. Nonetheless, any slowdown in profit 
growth, in an environment of a much higher cost of capital, could put downward 
pressure on equity prices, reducing confidence in future cash flows across the ecosystem 
from the hyperscalers to the ‘pick and shovels’ that have been benefiting from the capex 
boom.  
10. Given the near-term risks in bond markets, we remain neutral on equities, alongside 
other asset classes on a 3-month horizon, but continue to be overweight equities over 
12 months. While profit growth is likely to slow from the recent strong pace, ongoing 
growth in nominal GDP should underpin equity markets and the earnings cycle 
propelling equity returns even without any valuation expansion from current levels and 
our economists continue to see a low risk of recession. As our asset allocation team has 
shown, earnings growth and ROE are likely to continue to be the key drivers of equity 
performance relative to bonds, and we expect these fundamentals to remain supportive 
for equity returns. Nevertheless, we continue to recommend well diversified equity 
exposure as opportunities continue to broaden across geographies, sectors and factors.

## Document 332: Is AI Useless_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Is AI Useless_.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Is AI Useless?
Date:    15 minutes ago
Is AI Useless?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Is AI Useless?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 26 Oct 2026
View in browser

I. AI's Toxic Financial Trifecta
Recent AI jitters have been fueled by a potentially toxic trifecta of financial
constraints: elevated borrowing costs, uncertain investment returns, and
concerns about whether sufficient capital exists to finance everyone's
ambitious AI spending plans. Consider the following:
(1) Elevated borrowing costs. The 10-year Treasury yield is hovering
near the upper end of its "old normal" range of 4.0%-5.0% (chart). That
puts it at the highest level since 2007. Overall, credit spreads (the extra

### Page 2

yield paid over equivalent US Treasuries, such as the 10-year note) have
not blown out uniformly across the board. Instead, the market has
bifurcated sharply between the pristine balance sheets of mega-cap tech
companies and the leveraged balance sheets of pure-play
infrastructure/data center borrowers.
Yields on AI debt have not broadly surged relative to Treasuries for the
dominant Big Tech issuers, whose balance sheets continue to be treated
virtually like "proxy sovereigns." However, the credit market is increasingly

imposing credit discipline, demanding significantly higher risk premiums
and spreads from the leveraged second- and third-tier infrastructure
borrowers that are building out the physical plumbing of AI. Increased
concerns about the credit quality of AI borrowers have been reflected in
widening credit default spreads (chart).

### Page 3

(2) Uncertain returns. Gartner estimates that AI data center operators
must generate $8.5 trillion in cumulative revenues through 2030 to cover
their investments and associated financing costs and still achieve a 12%
return on invested capital. Yet Gartner projects no more than $3.6 trillion in
attributable AI revenues over that period. In our view, if the numbers don't
add up, the pace of the AI buildout will slow. That wouldn’t be a calamity.
(3) Increasingly constrained capital. Meta's Q3 earnings report

intensified concerns about capital availability. Despite strong revenue
growth, the company reported negative free cash flow for the first time
since 2012 and announced new non-voting shares to facilitate fundraising,
warning of an "increasingly difficult fundraising environment." Today, Meta
unveiled plans to raise up to $95 billion through equity offerings.
Meanwhile, Broadcom CEO Hock Tan identified capital as potentially the
biggest constraint on AI development. BlackRock CEO Larry Fink

described competition for capital as the fiercest of his career, while Nvidia
CEO Jensen Huang observed that neocloud demand is "off the charts,"
but capital is scarce.
Today, Elon Musk warned that more than 200 specialized AI cloud
providers (i.e., neoclouds) are headed for a wave of "guppy-level death
and consolidation." Musk stated that debt markets have "almost entirely
shut off access to money for any company that doesn't have substantial
revenue independent of AI data centers." In recent weeks, the neocloud

providers’ stock prices have tumbled (chart).

### Page 4

On balance, hyperscalers’ stock prices have held up relatively well (chart).
II. Will AI Pay Off?
Demand for compute nevertheless remains extraordinary. Global
spending on cloud infrastructure services surged 43% y/y to $143 billion in
Q2, its fastest growth in eight years. But how sustainable is that demand
growth? Ultimately, the marginal economic benefits derived from AI must
justify the marginal cost of supplying the necessary compute.
We believe AI is already generating tangible economic benefits, even if

they have yet to become clearly visible in aggregate macroeconomic
statistics. Consider the following:
(1) Anthropic's S-1. Anthropic’s IPO filing today offered five revealing
takeaways: First, revenues soared from $1.2 billion in H1-2025 to $16.3
billion in H1-2026. Second, operating losses narrowed from $2.1 billion to
$693 million, with Q2 marking the company's first quarterly operating profit
of $974 million. Third, revenue per dollar of compute expense surged 5.2-

fold to $2.08 between Q1-2025 and Q2-2026. Fourth, Anthropic held
$131.7 billion in cash and short-term investments as of June, alongside
$3.9 billion in H1 operating cash flow. Fifth, its enterprise footprint
expanded rapidly, with more than 11,900 customers spending at least
$100,000 annually as of September, up from roughly 950 a year earlier.
(2) Corporate America's emerging AI payoffs. An August Janus
Henderson study of 109 companies found that 69% reported some margin

### Page 5

improvement associated with productivity gains and AI-related cost
reductions. Among the examples cited, Shell reported $400 million in
annual savings and a 45% reduction in unplanned downtime; Freeport-
McMoRan increased mill throughput 10% while cutting unit costs by up to
15%; inventories at one Hitachi facility fell 50% and lead times shortened
by 77%; and IBM reported $4.5 billion in AI-related savings in 2025.
Last Thursday, Barclays announced plans to eliminate approximately

5,000 jobs, or 5% of its workforce, as AI and broader technological
modernization improve efficiency. PayPal is targeting $1.5 billion in
annualized savings, partly through AI adoption.
(3) Corporate profit margins. Corporate America is becoming more
profitable. The forward profit margin of the S&P 500 excluding its
Information Technology sector has been climbing this year, reaching
12.9% in October (chart). Excluding just the Semiconductors industry, the

forward profit margin rose to a record 14.4%. The forward profit margin in
Semiconductors rose to a record 51.8% in the week of October 23. But
most of the industry’s margin expansion since 2024 is attributable to
capacity constraints pushing prices higher.
A closer look reveals encouraging trends in industries where AI
applications could be particularly beneficial. Forward profit margins have
expanded in numerous financial industries where AI offers significant

opportunities for automation (chart).

### Page 6

Health Care stands to benefit from AI applications that can streamline
clinical workflows and administrative processes (chart).
(4) Bottom line. We aren't yet seeing significant AI-driven profit margin
expansion across the economy, but anecdotal evidence of AI-related
productivity gains is mounting. We expect AI adoption to become
increasingly widespread across industries, eventually delivering
substantial productivity growth and boosting corporate profit margins. AI's

economic payoff may not yet be obvious in aggregate statistics, but that
doesn't mean it isn't already materializing.

## Document 383: Swing State_ Navigating Midterm Volatility (2026-10-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Swing State_ Navigating Midterm Volatility (2026-10-23).pdf
Report source: DB Research

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank

Swing State: Navigating 
Midterm Volatility
